  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---

  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Bob: I think Noah's suspicion is misplaced. Leo and I were both quiet last night because we were focused on the game, not because we're hiding anything. The werewolves are among us, but pointing fingers without evidence could lead us astray. Let's keep our focus on logic and not just behavior.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t killed by the werewolf. I’m not sure who the werewolf is yet, but I believe we should focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. Let’s keep an open mind and use the information we have wisely.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (9): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously. Who else could be a threat?
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Bob: I think Noah's suspicion is misplaced. Leo and I were both quiet last night because we were focused on the game, not because we're hiding anything. The werewolves are among us, but pointing fingers without evidence could lead us astray. Let's keep our focus on logic and not just behavior.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t killed by the werewolf. I’m not sure who the werewolf is yet, but I believe we should focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. Let’s keep an open mind and use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the most to gain from Quinn's death or who showed inconsistent behavior. Leo being quiet isn’t enough on its own, but if others notice similar patterns or contradictions in their stories, that could help. Let’s keep track of everyone’s actions and statements carefully.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: As a member of the werewolf side, I believe we need to stay calm and avoid drawing attention to ourselves. While some may suspect Leo, we must ensure our actions remain subtle. Let’s keep the discussion focused on logic and not let emotions cloud our judgment.

[ACTION={'statement': 'As a member of the werewolf side, I believe we need to stay calm and avoid drawing attention to ourselves. While some may suspect Leo, we must ensure our actions remain subtle. Let’s keep the discussion focused on logic and not let emotions cloud our judgment.'}  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Bob: I think Noah's suspicion is misplaced. Leo and I were both quiet last night because we were focused on the game, not because we're hiding anything. The werewolves are among us, but pointing fingers without evidence could lead us astray. Let's keep our focus on logic and not just behavior.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t killed by the werewolf. I’m not sure who the werewolf is yet, but I believe we should focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. Let’s keep an open mind and use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the most to gain from Quinn's death or who showed inconsistent behavior. Leo being quiet isn’t enough on its own, but if others notice similar patterns or contradictions in their stories, that could help. Let’s keep track of everyone’s actions and statements carefully.
  Zoe: As a member of the werewolf side, I believe we need to stay calm and avoid drawing attention to ourselves. While some may suspect Leo, we must ensure our actions remain subtle. Let’s keep the discussion focused on logic and not let emotions cloud our judgment.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (9): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously. Who else could be a threat?
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Bob: I think Noah's suspicion is misplaced. Leo and I were both quiet last night because we were focused on the game, not because we're hiding anything. The werewolves are among us, but pointing fingers without evidence could lead us astray. Let's keep our focus on logic and not just behavior.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t killed by the werewolf. I’m not sure who the werewolf is yet, but I believe we should focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. Let’s keep an open mind and use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the most to gain from Quinn's death or who showed inconsistent behavior. Leo being quiet isn’t enough on its own, but if others notice similar patterns or contradictions in their stories, that could help. Let’s keep track of everyone’s actions and statements carefully.
  Zoe: As a member of the werewolf side, I believe we need to stay calm and avoid drawing attention to ourselves. While some may suspect Leo, we must ensure our actions remain subtle. Let’s keep the discussion focused on logic and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to be cautious about jumping to conclusions. While Leo's silence might raise questions, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. As a villager, my focus is on using logic and the information we have. Let’s analyze everyone’s behavior and statements carefully before making a decision.

[ACTION={'statement': "I think we need to be cautious about jumping to conclusions. While Leo's silence might raise questions, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. === Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (9): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously. Who else could be a threat?
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Bob: I think Noah's suspicion is misplaced. Leo and I were both quiet last night because we were focused on the game, not because we're hiding anything. The werewolves are among us, but pointing fingers without evidence could lead us astray. Let's keep our focus on logic and not just behavior.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t killed by the werewolf. I’m not sure who the werewolf is yet, but I believe we should focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. Let’s keep an open mind and use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the most to gain from Quinn's death or who showed inconsistent behavior. Leo being quiet isn’t enough on its own, but if others notice similar patterns or contradictions in their stories, that could help. Let’s keep track of everyone’s actions and statements carefully.
  Zoe: As a member of the werewolf side, I believe we need to stay calm and avoid drawing attention to ourselves. While some may suspect Leo, we must ensure our actions remain subtle. Let’s keep the discussion focused on logic and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Tara: I think we need to be cautious about jumping to conclusions. While Leo's silence might raise questions, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. As a villager, my focus is on using logic and the information we have. Let’s analyze everyone’s behavior and statements carefully before making a decision.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. While some may suspect Leo, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. Let’s analyze everyone’s actions and statements carefully before making a decision.

=== Werewolf — Day 4 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (6): Mia, Noah, Bob, Paul, Jack, Tara

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Zoe (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Iris (was Doctor) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously. Who else could be a threat?
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Bob: I think Noah's suspicion is misplaced. Leo and I were both quiet last night because we were focused on the game, not because we're hiding anything. The werewolves are among us, but pointing fingers without evidence could lead us astray. Let's keep our focus on logic and not just behavior.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t killed by the werewolf. I’m not sure who the werewolf is yet, but I believe we should focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. Let’s keep an open mind and use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the most to gain from Quinn's death or who showed inconsistent behavior. Leo being quiet isn’t enough on its own, but if others notice similar patterns or contradictions in their stories, that could help. Let’s keep track of everyone’s actions and statements carefully.
  Zoe: As a member of the werewolf side, I believe we need to stay calm and avoid drawing attention to ourselves. While some may suspect Leo, we must ensure our actions remain subtle. Let’s keep the discussion focused on logic and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Tara: I think we need to be cautious about jumping to conclusions. While Leo's silence might raise questions, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. As a villager, my focus is on using logic and the information we have. Let’s analyze everyone’s behavior and statements carefully before making a decision.
  Leo: I think we need to focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. While some may suspect Leo, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. Let’s analyze everyone’s actions and statements carefully before making a decision.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Mia: As the Seer, I investigated Quinn and confirmed they are a Villager. However, I also investigated Zoe last night and found her to be a Werewolf. This means Zoe is part of the werewolf side. We must be cautious and consider her actions during the discussion. Zoe’s statements should be scrutinized, as she is likely trying to mislead us. Let’s use this information to make an informed vote.
  Noah: Mia revealed that Zoe is a Werewolf, which explains her earlier comments about staying calm and avoiding suspicion. Zoe’s role as a werewolf means she was likely trying to manipulate the discussion. We need to vote her out immediately to protect the village. Let’s act on this clear evidence and eliminate Zoe.
  Bob: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to be cautious. Zoe is a werewolf, but accusing her openly could alert the remaining werewolf and make them more careful. Let’s continue observing and use logic to ensure we don’t let the real threat slip away.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I saved someone last night, and they are still alive. With Mia confirming Zoe is a Werewolf, we have clear evidence. Voting Zoe out now is the logical step to protect the village and prevent further harm. Let’s act decisively.
  Paul: Mia has confirmed Zoe is a Werewolf, so voting her out is the logical and necessary step. We cannot afford to let her continue influencing the discussion. Let’s eliminate Zoe to protect the village.
  Zoe: Mia has confirmed Zoe is a Werewolf, and I agree we need to act on this evidence. However, I believe we should also consider that there may be another werewolf among us. Bob and I are the only ones left from the werewolf side, and I suspect Bob might be the other. Let’s keep our focus on logic and not let our emotions dictate our next move.
  Jack: Mia confirmed Zoe is a Werewolf, so voting her out is the logical next step. However, Zoe's claim that Bob might also be a Werewolf raises a red flag. Bob was quick to dismiss suspicion from the start and has remained calm despite being a potential target. We need to verify Bob's alignment before making assumptions. Let's focus on eliminating Zoe first, then re-evaluate Bob's actions in the next round.
  Tara: Mia has confirmed Zoe is a Werewolf, so voting her out is the logical next step. However, Zoe's claim that Bob might also be a Werewolf raises a red flag. Bob was quick to dismiss suspicion from the start and has remained calm despite being a potential target. We need to verify Bob's alignment before making assumptions. Let's focus on eliminating Zoe first, then re-evaluate Bob's actions in the next round.
  --- Day 4 discussion ---
  Mia: I investigated Quinn, Zoe, and Bob. Quinn is a Villager, Zoe is a Werewolf, and Bob is also a Werewolf. With Zoe's identity confirmed, we must vote her out immediately. However, Bob’s calm demeanor and quick dismissal of suspicion make him a prime suspect as the remaining werewolf. Let’s eliminate Zoe now and keep a close eye on Bob for the next night.
  Noah: Mia has confirmed that both Zoe and Bob are Werewolves. Since Zoe is already in the discussion, we must vote her out immediately. However, Bob’s consistent calmness and early dismissal of suspicion make him the most likely remaining threat. Let’s eliminate Zoe now and prepare to target Bob next.
  Bob: As a Werewolf, I need to ensure my survival. Mia has already confirmed that Bob is a Werewolf, which means I must avoid being targeted. I will continue to act calmly and logically, aligning with the group's reasoning to avoid suspicion. Let’s focus on eliminating Zoe first, as she is already identified as a threat.
  Paul: Mia has confirmed that both Zoe and Bob are Werewolves. While Zoe is already in the discussion, we must act swiftly to eliminate her before she can influence the group further. However, Bob’s calm demeanor and early dismissal of suspicion make him the most likely remaining threat. Let’s vote Zoe out now and keep a close eye on Bob for the next night.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously. Who else could be a threat?
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Bob: I think Noah's suspicion is misplaced. Leo and I were both quiet last night because we were focused on the game, not because we're hiding anything. The werewolves are among us, but pointing fingers without evidence could lead us astray. Let's keep our focus on logic and not just behavior.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t killed by the werewolf. I’m not sure who the werewolf is yet, but I believe we should focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. Let’s keep an open mind and use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the most to gain from Quinn's death or who showed inconsistent behavior. Leo being quiet isn’t enough on its own, but if others notice similar patterns or contradictions in their stories, that could help. Let’s keep track of everyone’s actions and statements carefully.
  Zoe: As a member of the werewolf side, I believe we need to stay calm and avoid drawing attention to ourselves. While some may suspect Leo, we must ensure our actions remain subtle. Let’s keep the discussion focused on logic and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Tara: I think we need to be cautious about jumping to conclusions. While Leo's silence might raise questions, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. As a villager, my focus is on using logic and the information we have. Let’s analyze everyone’s behavior and statements carefully before making a decision.
  Leo: I think we need to focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. While some may suspect Leo, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. Let’s analyze everyone’s actions and statements carefully before making a decision.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously. Who else could be a threat?
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Bob: I think Noah's suspicion is misplaced. Leo and I were both quiet last night because we were focused on the game, not because we're hiding anything. The werewolves are among us, but pointing fingers without evidence could lead us astray. Let's keep our focus on logic and not just behavior.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t killed by the werewolf. I’m not sure who the werewolf is yet, but I believe we should focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. Let’s keep an open mind and use the information we have wisely.

  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.

  Leo: I think we need to focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. While some may suspect Leo, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. Let’s analyze everyone’s actions and statements carefully before making a decision.

  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.

=== Werewolf — Day 5 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (5): Noah, Bob, Paul, Jack, Tara

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Zoe (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Iris (was Doctor) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 4: Mia (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously. Who else could be a threat?
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Bob: I think Noah's suspicion is misplaced. Leo and I were both quiet last night because we were focused on the game, not because we're hiding anything. The werewolves are among us, but pointing fingers without evidence could lead us astray. Let's keep our focus on logic and not just behavior.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t killed by the werewolf. I’m not sure who the werewolf is yet, but I believe we should focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. Let’s keep an open mind and use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the most to gain from Quinn's death or who showed inconsistent behavior. Leo being quiet isn’t enough on its own, but if others notice similar patterns or contradictions in their stories, that could help. Let’s keep track of everyone’s actions and statements carefully.
  Zoe: As a member of the werewolf side, I believe we need to stay calm and avoid drawing attention to ourselves. While some may suspect Leo, we must ensure our actions remain subtle. Let’s keep the discussion focused on logic and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Tara: I think we need to be cautious about jumping to conclusions. While Leo's silence might raise questions, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. As a villager, my focus is on using logic and the information we have. Let’s analyze everyone’s behavior and statements carefully before making a decision.
  Leo: I think we need to focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. While some may suspect Leo, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. Let’s analyze everyone’s actions and statements carefully before making a decision.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously. Who else could be a threat?
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Bob: I think Noah's suspicion is misplaced. Leo and I were both quiet last night because we were focused on the game, not because we're hiding anything. The werewolves are among us, but pointing fingers without evidence could lead us astray. Let's keep our focus on logic and not just behavior.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t killed by the werewolf. I’m not sure who the werewolf is yet, but I believe we should focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. Let’s keep an open mind and use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the most to gain from Quinn's death or who showed inconsistent behavior. Leo being quiet isn’t enough on its own, but if others notice similar patterns or contradictions in their stories, that could help. Let’s keep track of everyone’s actions and statements carefully.
  Zoe: As a member of the werewolf side, I believe we need to stay calm and avoid drawing attention to ourselves. While some may suspect Leo, we must ensure our actions remain subtle. Let’s keep the discussion focused on logic and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Tara: I think we need to be cautious about jumping to conclusions. While Leo's silence might raise questions, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death.   Leo: I think we need to focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. While some may suspect Leo, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. Let’s analyze everyone’s actions and statements carefully before making a decision.

=== Werewolf — Day 6 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (4): Noah, Bob, Paul, Jack

  Day 3: Zoe (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Iris (was Doctor) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 4: Mia (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 5: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously. Who else could be a threat?
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Bob: I think Noah's suspicion is misplaced. Leo and I were both quiet last night because we were focused on the game, not because we're hiding anything. The werewolves are among us, but pointing fingers without evidence could lead us astray. Let's keep our focus on logic and not just behavior.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t killed by the werewolf. I’m not sure who the werewolf is yet, but I believe we should focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. Let’s keep an open mind and use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the most to gain from Quinn's death or who showed inconsistent behavior. Leo being quiet isn’t enough on its own, but if others notice similar patterns or contradictions in their stories, that could help. Let’s keep track of everyone’s actions and statements carefully.
  Zoe: As a member of the werewolf side, I believe we need to stay calm and avoid drawing attention to ourselves. While some may suspect Leo, we must ensure our actions remain subtle. Let’s keep the discussion focused on logic and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Tara: I think we need to be cautious about jumping to conclusions. While Leo's silence might raise questions, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. As a villager, my focus is on using logic and the information we have. Let’s analyze everyone’s behavior and statements carefully before making a decision.
  Leo: I think we need to focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. While some may suspect Leo, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. Let’s analyze everyone’s actions and statements carefully before making a decision.

  Noah: Mia revealed that Zoe is a Werewolf, which explains her earlier comments about staying calm and avoiding suspicion.   Bob: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to be cautious. Zoe is a werewolf, but accusing her openly could alert the remaining werewolf and make them more careful. Let’s continue observing and use logic to ensure we don’t let the real threat slip away.

=== Werewolf — Day 7 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (3): Noah, Bob, Paul

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Zoe (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Iris (was Doctor) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 4: Mia (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 5: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 6: Jack (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously. Who else could be a threat?
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Bob: I think Noah's suspicion is misplaced. Leo and I were both quiet last night because we were focused on the game, not because we're hiding anything. The werewolves are among us, but pointing fingers without evidence could lead us astray. Let's keep our focus on logic and not just behavior.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t killed by the werewolf. I’m not sure who the werewolf is yet, but I believe we should focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. Let’s keep an open mind and use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the most to gain from Quinn's death or who showed inconsistent behavior. Leo being quiet isn’t enough on its own, but if others notice similar patterns or contradictions in their stories, that could help. Let’s keep track of everyone’s actions and statements carefully.
  Zoe: As a member of the werewolf side, I believe we need to stay calm and avoid drawing attention to ourselves. While some may suspect Leo, we must ensure our actions remain subtle. Let’s keep the discussion focused on logic and not let emotions cloud our judgment.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s consider him as a possible werewolf.
  Tara: I think we need to be cautious about jumping to conclusions. While Leo's silence might raise questions, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. As a villager, my focus is on using logic and the information we have. Let’s analyze everyone’s behavior and statements carefully before making a decision.
  Leo: I think we need to focus on logical reasoning rather than just behavior. While some may suspect Leo, we don’t have concrete evidence linking him to Quinn’s death. Let’s analyze everyone’s actions and statements carefully before making a decision.

  Bob: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to be cautious. Zoe is a werewolf, but accusing her openly could alert the remaining werewolf and make them more careful. Let’s continue observing and use logic to ensure we don’t let the real threat slip away.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I saved someone last night, and they are still alive. With Mia confirming Zoe is a Werewolf, we have clear evidence. Voting Zoe out now is the logical step to protect the village and prevent further harm. Let’s act decisively.
